# Skills · S · part 115

854 entries, session-checkpoint to session-export. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **session-checkpoint**: Write a small, timestamped snapshot of current state to .memory/checkpoints/ periodically throughout a session (not just at the end), so a session that ends abruptly — credit exhaustion, crash, closed terminal — has a recent, resumable state to bootstrap from. Use after completing a meaningful unit of work, before starting something risky or long-running, and periodically during extended sessions — not only when the user asks to "save" or "checkpoint." ([NITISH-R-G/agent-memory-protocol](https://github.com/NITISH-R-G/agent-memory-protocol/tree/HEAD/skills/session-checkpoint) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **session-checkpoint**: Write a compact continuation checkpoint to XDG state when context is high, preserving decisions, progress, blockers, and verification evidence with integrity-checked durable continuity. ([RB-chrismandich/Manifest](https://github.com/RB-chrismandich/Manifest/tree/HEAD/plugins/manifest-workspace/skills/session-checkpoint) · ★1)
- **session-checkpoint**: Use when starting or ending a project work session, switching sessions, or saving or restoring project handoff context. Reads and updates the local, ignored root `SESSION_CHECKPOINT.md`; it is a handoff note, not project history. For example, "help me pick up where I left off". Trigger on /session-checkpoint. ([sornbuen15/drunken-guild](https://github.com/sornbuen15/drunken-guild/tree/HEAD/skills/workflow/session-checkpoint) · ★1)

([tanujsutaria/VitalArc](https://github.com/tanujsutaria/VitalArc/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/session-checkpoint) · ★1)
- **session-checkpoint**: Write or update STATUS.md so work can be resumed cheaply after a session ends. - **session-checkpoint**: End of a task, not of the day: writes a hand-off block to '## In progress' in SESSION_STATE.md and a checkpoint sub-block in today's SESSION_LOG.md entry, then commits without pushing. Runs without asking when the next focus is in the same sentence. Not for ending the day (that is session-end).
